In a moody Buenos Aires bedroom, heavy burgundy velvet drapes block the afternoon light, leaving the space cocooned in a warm, sienna darkness pierced by a single intense blade of tropical sun cutting through jalousie blinds. The scene compresses into a palpable flatness through a 90mm telephoto lens, where a slow-turning wooden ceiling fan slices the top edge of the composition. On a dark mahogany bed with rumpled, deep gold and cream sheets, a 29-year-old Argentine woman kneels on all fours. She has a petite, toned dancer's build, her defined shoulder blades casting sharp shadows down her back.
